### clerk-auth.js

  clerk-auth.js        — ClerkAuth (2.103.0, env-gated: VIBESPACE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY = on-switch, frontend-API host + JWKS URL DERIVED from the key's base64 payload; VIBESPACE_CLERK_ALLOWED_EMAILS comma list, @domain = whole domain, EMPTY = reject all). verifyToken = pure node:crypto RS256 vs cached JWKS (1h TTL, unknown-kid rotation refetch, exp/nbf ±60s skew, iss check, alg pinned to RS256). REQUIRES an email claim in the token — Clerk dashboard: session-token custom claims {"email":"{{user.primary_email_address}}"} or a JWT template named 'vibespace' (login page tries template first). No Clerk SECRET key anywhere — the backend only reads JWKS

### agent-routes.js

  agent-routes.js      — setupAgentRoutes() (2.93.0 split): every agent-facing endpoint — vibespace-ask/status/task routes, task-context/prompt-context injection (incl. user preamble + per-turn extras), stop-check nudge arbiter. Injection ORDER + SIZE are load-bearing — read the renderContext notes before touching payloads. TEACHING ARCHITECTURE (2.111.22-25, user-driven): the injected tools section is a DISCOVERY layer — per tool one trigger sentence + one fenced COPY-READY complete invocation (status sample carries --reason+--detail+--urgency; progress/ask carry --detail) so the first copied call is already valid; syntax/edge cases live in each CLI's own no-args output; hard rules enforce at point of use (waiting-state reason+detail rejection, success-output reminders). Don't grow the upfront block back into a rules dump (2.68.0 oversize incident is the other guardrail). **GROUP UPDATES DELIVER AS DIFFS (2.113.0, user request):** a mid-session group change no longer re-injects the whole group context — the session keeps a per-group SNAPSHOT of what it last saw (`s._groupSnap`, set at every delivery next to `_groupSeenAt`/`_ctxSig`; in-memory, restart ⇒ full re-delivery like the seen markers) and prompt-context injects a `<vibespace-task-update>` DELTA block (tasks.renderContextDiff: renamed / objective / backlog PARKED-RESOLVED-DROPPED-CLAIMED-UNCLAIMED-reworded-REMOVED one-liners (2.122.0; since 2.123.0 matched by the stable item id — occurrence-indexed text pairing retired — and TARGETED: `sessionKey` filters events to items the session created or claimed, everyone else keeps only the count pointer) / contextDir files updated-new-removed via old-vs-new `_ctxSig` / new activity entries LAST; ~5KB hard cap with tail-first truncation + `show --full` pointers; EMPTY diff = a no-op edit ⇒ nothing injected, markers still advance — the old code re-sent everything for those too; checklist deltas REMOVED with the feature in 2.121.0). No snapshot / setting `agents.contextUpdateDiffs` off ⇒ the old full "was UPDATED" payload. Also: FIRST-TIME groups on one prompt get ONE layered renderMultiContext (the codex first-prompt path) instead of N full payloads each repeating the ~2.3KB tools section — but ONLY when they cover the WHOLE membership (renderMultiContext states absolute membership; a subset call told a 3-group session "belongs to 2" — partial sets render per-group, count-free). REVIEW-HARDENED invariants (10-finding adversarial pass, do not regress; the checklist occurrence-indexed-diff one retired with the feature in 2.121.0): contextDir set/changed/cleared ⇒ diff returns null ⇒ FULL fallback (structural — the file index + folder conventions must be taught, a one-line path left pre-existing files invisible); the TASK.md pointer renders for LOCAL sessions only (remote ctx rsync excludes `.vibespace/`); contextDirSignature escapes `%`/`|` in paths (raw `|` sheared parse entries → wrong-path reports); **N CHANGED GROUPS COLLAPSE INTO ONE COMBINED BLOCK (2.113.1, user directive twice-clarified — 穿插/layered, the 2.68.0 class):** stacked per-group `<vibespace-task-update>` blocks + the ~2KB persisted-preview truncation could hide the very FACT that a 2nd group changed — `renderContextDiffMulti` (fed by `diffChanges`, which returns {lines, bits} where bits = phrase summaries like '3 new activity') renders ONE block whose HEADER line enumerates EVERY changed group + its summary, then per-group `##` sections SMALLEST-FIRST, 6.5KB cap with tail-first truncation (details die, the enumeration never does); ANY multi-block delivery (diff block + full re-deliveries + new-group fulls) is manifest-headed — `<vibespace-delivery-note>` naming EVERY block by group + the persisted-output rescue, diff block first, fulls last; combined parts >8KB with no rescue line get one prepended; markers/snapshot advance at RENDER, so a harness-dropped delivery (hook 3s timeout) stays lost until `show --full`/restart — ACCEPTED, same class as the seen-bump loss window. Tests: scripts/test-context-diff.mjs (store) + scripts/test-prompt-context.mjs (route, fake express)

### usage-routes.js

  usage-routes.js      — setupUsage() (2.92.0 split): the ENTIRE usage/rate-limit cluster from server.js — passive statusline ingest, read-only OAuth token, opt-in active poll, on-demand quota refresh, codex rollout-tail summarizer (§ban-safety rules apply — see kb-design-lessons §9). **Account-identity truth (2.114.1, real mixup report):** ~/.claude.json's oauthAccount goes STALE after a /login switch (config said fish.audio, token was actually gmail — popup looked like two subs swapped quotas). The on-demand ⟳ ALSO fetches `/api/oauth/claude_cli/roles` (same human-gated click, never scheduled) and bakes orgUuid/orgName/orgEmail into the usage cache; the global↔named link prefers org-uuid equality over config-email match (proven-different org BREAKS an email match); `globalLogin.{actualEmail,identityMismatch}` drive the popup's corrected label + amber warning; data/bin/vibespace-usage preserve-merges the org fields like scopedWeekly. Smoke: scripts/test-usage-link.mjs. **2.260.0 chat-mode freshness (B-7edc/B-292b): ⟳ PREFERS the CLI's own `get_usage` control channel** — a live LOCAL claude chat session billed to the refreshed account answers `get_usage` ITSELF (first-party fetch, = typing /usage there; probeUsageForAccountKey in server.js → vsu- request_id interception in the stdout parse → usage-cache write), human-gated + 60s throttle + silent fallback to the bare read-only call. **AUTO-firing get_usage is REJECTED (user decision 2026-08-09): a machine-initiated quota check is the banned automated-access pattern regardless of who makes the HTTP call.** The chat-mode PASSIVE signal is the LIMIT BANNER instead: the CLI's own "You've reached your … limit" chat record → ClaudeCodeAdapter.parseLimitBanner (5h/weekly/scoped-by-name; unknown→fiveHour = shortest self-heal) → markLimitBanner writes the bucket dead into usage-cache (keeps a known future reset, else bounded 5h/24h guess so the marker self-expires via reset-passed⇒full) + IMMEDIATELY maybePoolAutoSwitch — zero API calls, this is what makes pool auto-switch work for chat-only accounts. LIVE-VERIFIED get_usage envelope (2026-08-09, real session, ZERO inference — the CLI answers without any turn): payload at `control_response.response.response` (DOUBLE nesting); `utilization` = 0-100 INTEGERS (parser normalizes >1); NO `model_scoped` array — named nullable fields (`seven_day_opus`/`seven_day_sonnet`) + internal CODENAME buckets (no-resets_at codenames skipped); parseGetUsageResponse has the named-field fallback. Test: scripts/test-get-usage-parse.mjs (21, incl. the captured live fixture + all three live banner wordings). **2.266.1 IDENTITY-GROUP token resolution (user's correct logic: if the pool can BILL a conversation to the account, ⟳ must be able to READ its quota): the refresh route's token lookup walks every id in the target's identity group (usageIdentityAccountIds in server.js — org-merged accounts span `__global__` + the named sub, and whichever token a live session is refreshing is the fresh one; the other idles into expiry) and probeUsageForAccountKey matches any live session whose resolveUsageKey lands in the group (pooled sessions resolve to their real target, so a pooled conversation answers get_usage for the account it bills); the result always writes to the REQUESTED key's cache. 'No valid token' now means the whole identity truly has none.** **2.245.0: POST /api/usage/refresh `{host, account}` = host-HELD account quota — hosts.readRemoteSubOAuth reads the account's own `~/.vibespace/subs/<id>` token on the host (READ-ONLY, never refresh, same §ban-safety class), persists `usage-cache/host-<hid>-<aid>.json` (boot loader matches the `-(sub-…)` pattern FIRST — the plain `host-*` regex would swallow these files), served as /api/usage `hostAccounts` keyed `<hostId>:<accountId>`; throttle key `host:<hid>:<aid>`. The usage POPUP's Remote-hosts section is REMOVED — hosts + held accounts live in the Agents machine overview; the popup tail carries a "Full overview →" door.** 2.369.33: refreshViaCliPanel's cache merge projects WEEKLY resets (sevenDay + scopedWeekly) from the previous cache entry when the panel omits them (0% bucket): claude-quota.projectReset(prev, WEEK_SEC, now) + resetsAtEstimated:true (the popup shows ≈); the 5-hour bucket is never projected (its window starts with the first request). **2026-09-07 readings-by-slot: the SESSION-LESS producer.** refreshViaCliPanel has no session, so there is no credential slot to resolve — its identity IS the config dir the spawn was given, and `key` / `CLAUDE_SECURESTORAGE_CONFIG_DIR` / the file it writes back are ONE decision (now asserted in code + pinned by test-readings-attribution §7). That was already true; it is stated because it is the same rule every other producer now follows — *a reading is keyed by the credentials the producing process was handed* — and this one gets it for free. **`/api/usage` also carries `logins[acctId] = {state, usable, since}`** from src/login-state.js so a panel can date a signed-out member's last REAL reading instead of presenting foreign numbers as current.

**identityMismatch semantics (2.337.3, owner confusion report):** `_usageGlobalLink.email` comes from the GLOBAL ~/.claude.json oauthAccount — a field EVERY relocated dir login also stamps (CLAUDE_SECURESTORAGE_CONFIG_DIR relocates only the secret store; the 2.244.2 consequence). After any Manage-Agents account login the config identity is therefore residue, NOT the machine login. The token-derived orgEmail/orgUuid is the truth and drives display + linking. The UI treats the mismatch as an informational note (residue explanation, "no action needed") — NEVER as a warning telling the user to /login (that advice would trigger a needless real global login switch).

### opslog.js

  opslog.js            — Optional persistent ops log (2.115.0, env-gated no-op): VIBESPACE_OPSLOG_DIR → console tee into daily-rotated files + boot/exit/crash markers (VIBESPACE_OPSLOG_CEPHFS_* = server kernel-mounts the path-scoped subtree first, same mechanism as My storage). Built for fleets: logs survive pod recreation on shared storage the admin scans directly — deliberately NOT a logging stack (no per-node agents/log DB; user architecture decision 2026-07-13). Hung-mount-proof: async-only writes + 10s circuit breaker (one stuck write disables logging, never blocks the app — §2.108.3 threadpool lesson); bounded queue (2000 lines) drops on wedged disk. Helm opslog.* (secret mons/fsName/client/key/path, defaults u-<user>-opslog)

### incident.js

  incident.js          — FREEZE THE SCENE for a panic-button capture (2.239.0, the admin's correction: users troubleshoot their own problems — ssh/resume/kill — and destroy the evidence before anyone looks). captureLocal: process table, dtach socket stats, VERBATIM copies of session-meta + wrapper-meta (clobbered by every create/kill), claude lock files (deleted on CLI exit), and per referenced conversation the transcript IDENTITY (size+mtime+**sha256**+frozen 512KB tail — a later manual `--resume` double-write/fork becomes provable). captureRemote: one bounded probe per machine over hosts._hostShell (ssh AND dial) — remote process table, locks, keeper/agentd state, transcript stat+sha256+last timestamps, project dirs, disk, CLI versions. ALL read-only + bounded (child processes with timeouts — never node fs on a possibly-hung mount, §2.108.3; ≤6 hosts, ≤12 conversations, size-capped copies) and per-step try/caught so a dead host degrades the bundle instead of failing it. Runs ASYNC after the id is returned; `env.json.pending` marks a cut-off freeze. Test: scripts/test-incident.mjs (16 asserts incl. frozen-copy-survives-clobber). **B-8ebb (last row, codex rollouts in the freeze):** the transcript-identity loop no longer knows only claude's project dirs — after the claude scan it iterates the HARNESS REGISTRY (`require('./harnesses').list()`, lazy) and asks each `store.locate(cid)` where its conversation lives: codex rollouts (`rollout-*-<tid>.jsonl` / `.jsonl.zst`, the local tree or the remote-jsonl cache) get the same size+mtime+sha256 identity + frozen tail (`<cid>__<dir>.tail.jsonl[.zst]`, `harness:'codex'`, `compressed:true` for a .zst whose raw tail bytes are the evidence, not lines); claude's locate dedups against the scan. The remote probe is generated the same way — `buildRemoteTranscriptProbe(cids)` emits one `find <root> <findExpr> | probe_transcript` line per (conversation × harness) from each descriptor's `store.remoteFind(id)` (claude's expression byte-identical to before, codex's `$HOME/.codex/sessions … rollout-*<id>.jsonl(.zst)` added; `probe_transcript` is one shell function; `lastts` greps with -a for compressed bytes) and the versions line asks codex too. Never a backend id branch in incident.js — a third harness freezes by registering a store. Test: scripts/test-incident.mjs (+8: a codex rollout in the cache is fingerprinted + tail-frozen + tagged, the claude conversation still freezes once, remote probe lines per harness, no id branch).

### routes/persistence.js

  routes/persistence.js — …also **LAYOUT ROLLBACK POINTS (2.296.0, after a bug flattened a user's whole desktop layout)**: `writeLayouts` is the ONE choke point every layout write passes through, so it snapshots the PREVIOUS state into `data/layout-history/` whenever the desktop→window SHAPE changes (per-desktop sorted window ids; geometry drags and z-order churn deliberately ignored or one drag would evict every useful restore point). A destructive write therefore always leaves the last good shape on disk — the thing the incident lacked, where two EMPTIED desktops made even the pre-damage mapping unrecoverable. Bounded 40; **filenames carry a sequence suffix — a bare ISO stamp has ms resolution and a mass-resume burst lands inside one millisecond, silently overwriting the older entry i.e. destroying the pre-damage state exactly when it matters (caught by the bounded-history assertion)**. Legacy top-level `autoSave` counted too (instances without virtual desktops otherwise read "0 windows"). Routes GET /api/layout-history + POST /api/layout-history/:id/restore (id regex-validated; restoring writes through the same path so it is ITSELF undoable). UI: ⚙ → Restore a previous layout… (setup-flows `_showLayoutHistory`). Test: scripts/test-layout-history.mjs (11).

### lib/incident-recorder.js

  lib/incident-recorder.js — "Report a problem" panic button (2.238.0, the timezone-gap fix): ALWAYS-ON client ring buffers (clicks/coarse keys — typed TEXT never recorded, only a per-burst marker; ws message TYPES both directions, never payloads; console errors/warns) + on-demand full client snapshot → POST /api/incident → server adds its own scene (activeSessions digest, 30-min sysinfo history, in-memory server console ring (600 lines, wraps console.* in server.js), hosts digest, resume-breaker table + remote-discovery cache digest — the two conversation-disappeared-class facts that survive nowhere else, 2.238.1) → data/incidents/<inc-id>/bundle.json (+followup.json auto-appended 2min later; newest 30 kept). **snapshot.chatTraces (2.264.0, B-21bc user request): each open chat window's SCROLL TRACER ring tail ships automatically** — chat-view's `_trace()` is an always-on capped ring again (the 2.111.8 removal stubbed it AND the documented Ctrl+Shift+J dump, leaving viewport-jump reports blind): paging/trim/jump/runsRestore breadcrumbs + a coarse scroll sampler (>400px moves w/ pin state + wheelAgo — user-scroll vs programmatic-yank discriminator) + an unpin breadcrumb; positions/op tags only, never content. User relays the short id; admin reads the bundle. Entry: ⚙ → Report a problem…; GET /api/incidents lists. **SEMANTIC OP BREADCRUMBS (2.296.0): `window.__vsOp(name, data)` pushes into a 4th ring (`op`, cap 300) — for ops that RELOCATE OR REPLACE a window/session, the class where "what did the app just do to my layout" is the whole question. The pool-collapse incident left NO trace of its desktop reassignments (the action ring has clicks, the ws ring has message types; neither answers 'what moved these windows'), so the sequence had to be reconstructed by reading code. Wired at desktop-move / pool-cold-restart / resume-place. Names+ids only — the privacy rule is unchanged. The server bundle also carries the LAYOUT TABLE (window→desktop+sid) and the layout-history index, which is what turns a flattened layout from JSON surgery into one click.** PRIVACY: the note is the only free text — keep it that way. Test: scripts/test-incident.mjs

### telemetry.js

  telemetry.js         — Telemetry (2.84.0, local-first observability): monthly ndjson shards data/telemetry/events-YYYY-MM.ndjson of {ts,kind:error|event|boot|server-error|metric,name,detail,stack,version,ua,value}; summary() = grouped errors + byName/byDay/byVersion + metrics (n/p50/p95/max/last per numeric metric — client boot-to-ready-ms/js-heap-mb/dom-nodes/open-windows/longtask-* per-min aggregates, server srv-rss-mb/srv-heap-mb/srv-evloop-max-lag-ms/srv-live-sessions/srv-ws-clients/srv-subagent-watchers/srv-normalizer-msgs/srv-buffer-files + HTTP rolling window (reqs/avg/max + slow-request events, route sanitized to 3 segments) every 5min, srv-usage-scan-ms/srv-jsonl-parse-ms on their operations; client also ws-outage-ms/gap-slab-load-ms/history-render-ms/chat-search-ms/session-create-roundtrip-ms/upload-mbps — 2.94.0-2.95.0; deep modules report via the global.__vsMetric hook); optional batch forwarding to telemetry.forwardUrl with an anonymous instance id (data/telemetry/.instance-id). Client side = src/lib/telemetry-client.js (window.onerror/unhandledrejection + boot event + coarse feature events like window-open:<type>/session-create; installed in client.js BEFORE new App() so App-constructor boot crashes are captured — the 'silent blank page' class; 60-events/page-session cap, 5 per identical error). ⚙ → Diagnostics report… (app.js _openDiagnostics) renders GET /api/telemetry/summary as a blob-URL HTML page in the embedded browser. NAMES ONLY, never content — see settings telemetry.enabled/forwardUrl/forwardToken. COLLECTOR MODE (2.101.0): ingestRemote() writes forwarded batches to central-YYYY-MM.ndjson shards (per-record inst id, remote ts/version preserved via _clean(ev,{remote:true}) — clamped ±sane window so a bad sender clock can't scatter shards); centralSummary() = per-instance {total,errors,lastTs,versions} + errors grouped ACROSS instances; route gating lives in server.js (VIBESPACE_TELEMETRY_INGEST_TOKEN)

### manage-agents.js

    manage-agents.js   — installManageAgents(App): Manage-Agents dialog + Anthropic/ChatGPT account rosters/wizard (~790 lines, split from app.js). NOTE (2.178.0 redesign): account rows are FLEX (icon·name/email·usage·★·⋯), NOT the old shared subgrid — the roster renders into the ~260px rail panel too and the fixed grid tracks crushed the name column there. Test/Re-login-on-this-machine (2.332.0: existing-dir helper flow, attempt-id watcher — signed-out rows say 'Log in on this machine…')/Rename/email/Remove live in the ⋯ menu (showContextMenu — item property is `action`, NOT onClick, bit once); the four Add… buttons collapse into one '+ Add account…' menu on the roster header. **Roster order = TYPE, never add-order (2.268.5, user request): pool → subscription → API key, name-sorted within a type — the SAME comparator is inlined in the billing switcher (session-lifecycle.js) and the New Session account dropdown (app.js updateAcctRow); keep the three in sync (divergent ordering between surfaces reads as a shuffle). Since 2.369.18 BOTH rosters use the module-level `rosterSort` (codex pools gave the codex roster a type dimension) and the module-level `ROSTER_ICONS` (one POOL glyph for every backend); the pool ⋯ menu is ONE block, `_poolMenuItems(id, a, refresh)`, spliced in by both rosters — never copy it per backend.** Modal + rail panel share ONE stylesheet: _showAgentsDialog/openPluginsDialog add the modal body class to the {container}, and .acct-list is a container-type:inline-size context so `@container (max-width:300px)` wraps the usage donuts under the name. Backend header is single-line (strips the redundant '(Claude Code)' version suffix). 2.179.1 (real overflow report): the panel is FLAT (`.rail-panel .ob-backend` hairline sections; flex-wrap ONLY on non-roster rows — wrap on a COLUMN flex makes align-items:stretch fill the LINE not the container, the unshrinkable-221px bug); usage is width-adaptive (`.acct-usage-mini` = tightest-bucket pill, container query ≤340px swaps donuts→pill); every nowrap leaf (name/email/roster title/add button) is flex-shrinkable w/ ellipsis. Verified by headless screenshots + scrollWidth audits at 260/340/460px. **THREE-TAB LAYOUT (2.262.0, user: 平铺缺分级+指令孤立): refresh() branches on localStorage `vibespace.agentsTab` — Accounts (default; the LOCAL section = management home) / Machines (one `<details class=agents-mach-acc>` accordion card per host, probe runs LAZILY on first expand — opening the panel no longer fans probes to every machine; open-set in `vibespace.agentsMachOpen`, single host auto-expands) / Instructions (the agents-adv fields, own tab, expanded). Refresh-all shared by the first two tabs; smoke drives the real tab navigation. FIXTURE LESSON: the 2.258.0 menu redesign moved 'Fa 41%' spacing from text to CSS gap and the smoke's /Fa \d+%/ text-regex went silently always-false — asserts that match textContent must tolerate CSS-provided spacing.** Pre-2.262.0 machine-sectioned notes still apply INSIDE each surface: **(2.245.0): the host selector is GONE — one `.agents-machine-sec` per host (`.usage-section-title` headers, per-machine `renderMachine(body=section, selectedHost, hostLabel, …)` fills in PARALLEL); host sections list VERDICT-usable accounts only (B-f531 — never client-computed; unreachable host says "unreachable", never "not installed"+Install) + the host CLI-login row; the LOCAL section stays the FULL roster (management home). Per-row usage follows verdict.how (host-login→hosts quota / host-held→`hostAccounts['<hid>:<aid>']` / else local passive cache). Header ⟳ "Refresh all" = `_refreshAllQuota` per-target fan-out (click-only §ban-safety, Anthropic calls STAGGERED ~1.5s — burst 429 risk §9; failures inline per-row `.acct-refresh-err`, never silent, so NO full refresh after). The codex roster's bare `racct` was a latent ReferenceError killing it on every host view (swallowed by try/catch) — fixed. **DONUT-COLUMN INVARIANT (2.245.2, pixel-measured regression): the usage cluster is right-anchored against `.acct-key-actions`, so EVERY row must carry the same [★][⋯] icon pair (min-width 48px belt for star-only rows) — row-specific actions (host ⟳/login/import) go in the ⋯ menu, NEVER inline (each inline button shifts that row's donuts left); `.acct-refresh-err` is one ellipsized flex-basis:100% line.** Smoke: scripts/test-agents-overview.mjs (36 asserts incl. the ±1px alignment sweep at 3 widths + a labelHtml escaping check). **POOLED ROW (2.263.0, real report '池化被当API账号渲染+菜单有查看key'): `isPool` branch — dedicated POOL icon (overlapping circles, never KEY), iconTitle names pooling, the usage cell shows the CURRENT TARGET's donuts (+dead-reckoned est), and 'Show key…' is gated `!isSub && !a?.pooled`. 2.267.1 finished the sweep: the billing SWITCHER row (`a.pooled` branch — `名 → target` + target's usage/est; remote = disabled local-only) and the New Session dropdown (pool fell into the API-key else-branch as '— API key …undefined') stopped mislabeling pools too; sessionAuth already emits source:'pooled'. Backend header nuance (2.268.4: with namedLoggedIn>0 the row's PRIMARY 'Log in' button hides — two look-alike green primaries next to '+ Add account…' — and the machine-wide login moves into the Add-account menu as 'Log in machine-wide (claude /login)…'; fresh machines keep the button): /api/backend-status carries `namedLoggedIn` (usable named identities) + `machineLoginState` (2.267.4: 'expired' = creds file present but token-less — worded 已失效, never claims idle; absent = never-set-up — worded 未配置机器登录) so a token-less MACHINE login under full pooling renders an honest two-state chip + 'N named account(s) in use' instead of the misleading '未登录'; mobile: the worst-of quota chip includes scoped weeklies (est-aware) and the window-switcher billing chip names pool → target.** `_acctUsageHtml(u, est)` renders estimates as a light conic arc + dashed outer ring (`.acct-donut-est`; pill fallback dashed-underlines) via utils.estDisplayPair — est<reading ⇒ window rolled ⇒ dark collapses to 0 (user-specified). **2.268.7: the age cell is a TWO-LINE micro-column — data age on top, reset countdown of the row's tightest bucket below (est-aware pick, bucket-pressure color; per-donut + pill tooltips carry per-bucket 'resets in …'); `.acct-usage-age` min-width 6.5ch fits the widest countdown so the donut-column alignment invariant holds — conditional/variable-width rendering there shifts donuts per row (the measured-28px-jump class).**

### resume-continuity.js (PURE, B-6b6d)

  src/resume-continuity.js — **THE RESUME LADDER**: `resumeSpawnPick({explicit, conversation, instanceDefault, resume, hasSource})` → `{value, origin}` with `origin ∈ chosen|conversation|instance|harness`, plus `continuityLogLine`. The owner's 2026-09-07 ruling in one function: **a conversation's OWN model/effort wins on resume/fork/restart; the instance default is a NEW-session default; an explicit per-session pick beats both.** TWO callers, one law — the CLIENT (session-lifecycle `createSession`, `hasSource:false` = only the server can read a conversation's records ⇒ a continuation sends NOTHING) and the SERVER (ws-create, immediately before `buildSessionArgs`, with the harness descriptor's `store.lastTurn<Knob>` reader wired in). `hasSource` is DERIVED from that hook's presence — never a parallel caps boolean. **`''` is a STATED choice, `undefined` is silence, and the difference only survives on a NEW session** (round 2): the New Session dialog's first option IS `''` ("Auto (model default)"), so collapsing it made an explicit Auto resolve to the instance default; on a non-continuation `''` wins as `{value:'', origin:'chosen'}` and the client sends the resolved value verbatim so it survives the wire. On a CONTINUATION the create message carries `model: sessionModel || undefined`, so there an explicit Auto and an absent field really are identical bytes and both mean "take the conversation's own value". A knob with NO source sends nothing on a resume rather than the default (an unconditional default there IS the defect); a source that came up EMPTY falls back to the default and the caller LOGS it. **`applyOriginHint(origin, hint)` (round 3)** — the wire's other half. On a NEW session the CLIENT resolves `<prefix>.default*` (it needs the value for its own chip, and it owns the legacy `session.defaultEffort` key + `settings.isModified`, which the server cannot see) and sends it VERBATIM, so the server saw a bare string it could only read as a pick: `_modelOrigin`/`_effortOrigin` were ALWAYS `'chosen'` for a new session, `'instance'`/`'harness'` were unreachable there, and Session Properties called `<prefix>.defaultModel` “your choice for this session” on every create path that shows no picker (openShellTerminal, the toolbar, setup-flows, manage-agents). The client now also sends the origin it computed (`spawnOriginHint`) and this reconciles: it may ONLY downgrade the server's own `'chosen'` to `'instance'`/`'harness'`, never upgrade or re-point a rung the server decided from facts the client never had, and it never touches the VALUE; a `'conversation'`/`'chosen'`/garbage hint is ignored, and an older client that sends none keeps master's answer. Gate: scripts/test-codex-effort-meta.mjs legs ⑪ / ⑫ / ⑫ c.

### session-lifecycle.js

    session-lifecycle.js — installSessionLifecycle(App): create/attach/resume/fork/view/kill + billing switcher + replayOpenSpec (~680 lines, split from app.js) ****2.369.8 restartConversationInPlace(sessLike)** = one-click Terminate+Resume (pool cold-switch machinery: kill → exited(+400ms flush) → resumeSession same cid, excludeWebuiId; dead sessions skip straight to resume; per-session config rides the respawn). Exposed on: style-menu pending row (chat-status-bar onRestartSession), window-title right-click (taskbar showWindowContextMenu session block: Restart/Terminate/Resume/Locate in sidebar/Session properties), sidebar card menu (live). locateSessionInSidebar = Folders panel + expand collapsed group + scrollIntoView + .locate-flash. RESUME PRESERVES THE HOME DESKTOP (2.295.0, a fleet user, inc-mso43urh): `_snapshotWinBounds` captures `_desktopId` and createSession moves the resumed window back to it (guarded on existence, never onto Stage) — without it a POOL cold-switch (which kill+resumes sessions across EVERY desktop at once while one is active) piled the whole layout onto the active desktop. A single billing switch is unchanged (home == active). Test: scripts/test-resume-desktop.mjs.** **THE INSTANCE DEFAULT IS A NEW-SESSION DEFAULT (B-6b6d, 2026-09-07):** `createSession` resolves model+effort through the PURE `resumeSpawnPick` (src/resume-continuity.js) with `hasSource:false`, so a create carrying a `resumeId` — resume bar, resume-all, a card with no ⚙ override, restartConversationInPlace, fork, and the two failed-resume retries (which re-send the caller's ORIGINAL explicitness, never the resolved values) — sends NO model/effort and the server's ladder gives the conversation its own back. `permission`/`extraArgs` deliberately keep the old behaviour (owner gate). The `created` handler applies `msg.effort` over the local `sessionEffort` for the same reason 2.368.4 exists: on a resume the client commanded nothing, so the server's resolved value is the only one there is. **Round 3:** `pickHere` keeps the WHOLE pick, not just its value, and `createMsg` carries `spawnOriginHint: {model, effort}` — on a NEW session this is the only place that knows whether the string on the wire is something the user stated or something the client resolved out of `<prefix>.default*`, and the server cannot tell them apart (src/resume-continuity.js `applyOriginHint`). The VALUE is unchanged: the client keeps resolving because it owns the legacy `session.defaultEffort` key and `settings.isModified`.

### sidebar-mounts.js

    sidebar-mounts.js  — Remote tab: machines (ssh hosts) + storage (mounts) rendering (installSidebarMounts mixin, ~1000 lines; 2.152.0: "Pair a device" action + _showDevicePairDialog — dial-out device pairing, mints via POST /api/agentd/dial-pair and renders the full installer command incl BOTH --dial-token and --host-token; the host token is what the daemon verifies OUR mux hello against — omit it and the device dials in but rejects every command). **KEY IMPORT (2.246.0): `_askPrivateKey({text,error,showPass})` is the reusable, PREFILLABLE "Private key" sub-dialog** — resolves {privateKey, keyPassphrase} or null; the Add-machine POST wraps it in a RETRY LOOP (a KEY_ERR_CODES failure re-opens it with the key still pasted + the localized reason, so a wrong passphrase is never a dead end). **TWO ORDERING TRAPS, both real: (1) `createModalShell`'s close() fires onClose on EVERY path, so the submit handler must `resolve(value)` BEFORE `ctx.close()` — close-then-resolve lets the cancel-resolver win and "Use this key" behaves exactly like Cancel (the entire paste-a-key flow was dead; idempotency alone does NOT save you, the FIRST settle wins; caught only by the headless dialog test). (2) sub-dialogs REUSE the overlay id `mounts-dialog-overlay`, and createModalShell REMOVES the existing one — so from the first sub-dialog onward the Add-machine dialog is DETACHED and _mountsDialog's inline error element renders into nothing; the submit callback takes `err` and toasts when `!err.isConnected`.** Passphrase + key text are read RAW from the elements (a passphrase may legitimately have leading/trailing whitespace — verified ssh-keygen accepts `'p@ss w/ spaces '`), deliberately bypassing _mountsDialog's trimming field reader. Detection reveals affordances but NEVER blocks submit (server is authoritative; an always-available "Key has a passphrase?" toggle covers a misjudged format), and the passphrase row uses `style.display`, not a `hidden` class (no global `.hidden` rule in this project)

### sidebar-rail.js

    sidebar-rail.js    — Sidebar activity rail (2.176.0, vscode-style, setting `sidebar.activityRail` default ON): installSidebarRail mixin — ~44px icon strip replaces the 3-tab bar; panels Folders/Tasks/Remote reuse the tab render paths, NEW panel tabs ports/agents/plugins/system render into the list area via _renderRailPanel (agents/plugins reuse the modal renderers through a `{container}` option on _showAgentsDialog/openPluginsDialog — those redirect to the rail panel when it exists, so ⚙-menu entries focus the panel); Ports panel = active forwards (open-via-proxy/publish/unpublish/stop) + per-machine scans, live on port-forwards-updated/machine-ports-new; badges (tasks ⚠ rides refreshTaskAttention, mounts=offline dial hosts, ports=forward count, diagnostics=1-day error count, one probe per page load); re-click active item = sidebar collapse; setting-off live-restores the classic tabs (wrapper .sidebar-main stays); mobile never renders it. 2.177.0: `sidebar.railPersistent` (default ON) — collapse keeps the 44px strip (`.rail-collapsed` class, width !important beats the resizer inline style; _applySidebarLayoutWidth reserves 44px; rail click while collapsed EXPANDS, never re-collapses); header `.sidebar-title` names the active panel (RAIL_TITLES); `.rail-panel` compact CSS lifts the agents modal min-width + wraps account rows; ports actions are error-surfaced (fetchJson never throws — check `r?.error`) + frp-unconfigured publish is dimmed-with-tooltip instead of a silent no-op. 2.353.0 SCAN CACHE (owner report: every broadcast-driven re-render wiped the freshly scanned list): scan results live in `this._portScanCache` (Map hostId → ports; survives panel re-render AND reopen), `renderList()` replays the cache on rebuild with fresh forward state so forwarded/published chips update live, and a `machine-ports-new` broadcast MERGES its announced ports into the cache before re-rendering (the panel must show what the toast talked about) — never leave scan results DOM-only. **2.354.0 PROCESS MANAGER (`_buildProcManager`, the btop analogue, replaces the old top-8 list):** System panel gains a full interactive table — GET /api/sysinfo/procs on the same 5s tick (remote ~10s), sort chips CPU/MEM/PID/Name/Tree (tree = partial forest over the capped table, missing parents become roots), 150ms-debounced filter (name/user/pid), display cap 120 with an explicit "+ show all" expander, per-row CPU gradient + hot coloring, expand → full cmdline + pid/ppid/user/state/etime + actions (Terminate/Force kill behind showConfirmDialog danger, Pause/STOP↔Resume/CONT unconfirmed, Copy cmd) → POST /api/sysinfo/signal with outcome toasts (gone vs still-running honesty). STRUCTURE RULES: controls (search input + sort chips) build ONCE outside the re-rendered table zone — an input inside the 5s-swapped zone loses focus every tick; expansions keyed by pid in `this._prcExpanded` survive refreshes; a `seq` guard drops stale /procs responses after a host switch; `this._prcRefresh` is called by the host switcher for an immediate swap; every cmd/user/etime string is escHtml'd (they come from REMOTE machines — the stored-XSS law); the server's own row gets a "server" chip + kill refusal instead of buttons. `.prc-name` is a flex pair (`.prc-nm` ellipsizes, chips `flex:none`) so the server/paused/zombie chip survives narrow-panel truncation. CDP asserts live in test-sidebar-rail.mjs (system-panel section). 2.356.0 UX batch: PID column (dim, right-aligned); auto-refresh PAUSE toggle in the header (`this._prcPaused` — the interval's periodic call checks it, explicit refreshes (host switch, kill outcome, un-pause) bypass; count label turns amber); panel zone order = bars → history charts → process table (the table is long — charts stay visible); memory history chart auto-scales its Y axis to peak×1.2 capped at the limit (an axis pinned to the machine total drew real usage as a flatline — the CPU chart was already data-scaled). GOTCHA fixed alongside (settings.js): applyRemote skips the settings-updated echo while a local save is pending — the sender receives its own broadcast, and a set(false)→set(true) inside the 500ms debounce was PERMANENTLY reverted by the first save's stale echo. Smoke: scripts/test-sidebar-rail.mjs (worktree+CDP, 14 asserts)

### usage-window.js

    usage-window.js      — Usage dashboard window (2.61.0, ⚙ → Usage, window type 'usage', openSpec openUsage). **2.96.0: default view = configurable panel dashboard** (usage-dashboard.js: panel = metricS × dimension × chart — 2.97.0 multi-metric: `metrics:[]` checkbox editor; 2.99.3 split-series: panel.splitBy = a 2nd dimension (editor "Split series by") — line→one series per split key, bars→STACKED; data = server 2-D pivots (`?pivot=day:account` → aggregate({pivots}) → pivots['a:b'] rows, cells = same finalized bucket shape as group rows), `panelPivots(panels)` tells the window's fetch which crosses to request and onChange/preset-apply refetch when a new one appears; top-6 + Other, labels from groups[splitBy] meta; Chart.js GOTCHA: every dataset MUST set yAxisID/xAxisID to the configured scale or a phantom default axis renders, Chart.js v4 (modular ~150KB, the ONLY chart lib since 2.98.0 — line/bar/doughnut all share one interaction model: formatted hover tooltips, legend series-toggling; bars auto-orient by dimension type; per-unit auto dual axes; instances MUST be destroyed via destroyCharts() before canvas removal — global registry + ResizeObserver leak otherwise; panels attach to the document BEFORE charts render or CSS-var color probes resolve black); 2.99.1: `.usage-body` is a container-type:inline-size container — `@container ≤700px` folds `.udash-grid` to 1 column so the layout tracks the WINDOW width (`.udash-add` spans `1 / -1`, NEVER `span 2` — in a 1-col grid `span 2` forces an implicit second track and the whole grid overflows); 2.99.2 h-scrollbar elimination invariants (verified 0 overflow 420–1100px, both views — do not regress): grid tracks `minmax(0,1fr)` never bare `1fr` (grid items default min-width:auto → one wide stat/table blows the track), `.udash-panel` is min-width:0 + its OWN container-type:inline-size (stat font `clamp(14px,10cqw,30px)` scales with the panel), `.udash-panel-body` overflow-x:auto contains table scroll, `.usage-seg` wraps, classic `.usage-grid` floors at `min(340px,100%)`, `.usage-body` overflow-x:hidden as the final guarantee; per-panel ✎ editor + ⋯ menu, presets, layout persisted in settings 'usage.dashboard'; pre-2.96 fixed layout = 'Classic view' escape hatch). Reads /api/usage-stats: headline tiles (est API-equivalent cost / total tokens = the 4 peer component tiles cached-read+cache-write+fresh-input+output / cache-hit-ratio / requests), daily-trend canvas bar chart, breakdowns by billing-type/account(type badges)/model/project/mode/cache-efficiency/hour/weekday/top-sessions, range filter (+ Custom… from/to date pickers, 2.63.0) + backend filter + ACCOUNT filter chips (2.63.0 — All/each account/CLI login; chip options UNION across loads so a narrow range doesn't drop chips; when app._usageGlobal.accountId links the CLI login to a named account the two render as ONE chip whose key is the comma pair), CSV export, + a built-in Pricing editor (tier list from pricing.tiers keys, per-account discount%, PATCH /api/usage-stats/pricing). Cost = estimate (subscriptions plan-covered). **DEVICE filter (2.128.0, user directive "hosts are devices, not accounts")**: a top-level 设备 row (All/This machine/each remote host) gates the WHOLE view via aggregate hostFilter (/api/usage-stats `host` param, matches ev.host||'local'); host buckets (type 'host') are EVICTED from the Account chips, and selecting a remote device hides the Account row entirely (remote usage = the host's own login) + clears any account filter. Remote data arrives via the 2.127.0 harvest (see data/bin/vibespace-usage-scan entry); harvest is CONSUMING (remote cursor advances) — tests must use VIBESPACE_USAGE_CURSOR temp paths, never production cursors (real incident: an e2e force-harvest ate the increment into a throwaway ledger and the UI looked unimplemented; rid-dedup made the cursor-reset re-harvest lossless)

### desktop-manager.js

    desktop-manager.js — DesktopManager (virtual desktops, Ubuntu-style previews, window routing) **DRAG-REORDER (2.250.0): desktop preview wrappers are draggable(text/desktop-id); dropping onto another preview → reorderDesktop → ws `desktop-reorder {order}` → server reconciles the id list against the stored set (unknown ids dropped, omitted appended, size-mismatch rejected) so a stale client can't drop/invent a desktop. The preview stays a DROP target for BOTH a window-move(text/window-id) and a reorder — resolved by which dataTransfer key is set. Right-click menu anchors by viewport half (top-docked taskbar put previews near the top where an unconditional bottom-anchor grew off-screen). ### stage-manager.js

    stage-manager.js   — StageManager (2.112.0 dynamic desktop 'Stage', default OFF via desktop.dynamicEnabled — docs/design-dynamic-desktop.md is the blueprint+progress anchor. VIEW model: one window two geometries (home gridBounds vs shared slot); materialization intercepts wm.focusWindow (_stageBypass prevents recursion; createWindow's tail focus flows through it too); per-hero aux workspaces = {openSpec, stageBounds, extras(scroll walker)} in SyncStore 'stage' (slot/ws:<key>/lru), heroKey RE-DERIVED AT RECORD TIME (backendSessionId arrives late); placeholder is a REAL wm window type 'stage-placeholder' — onWindowCreated MUST check the TYPE, the _isStagePlaceholder flag lands after createWindow's tail hook (smoke-caught: placeholder got swept as transient); incoming hero z-stacks at BOTTOM (moved slot must not cover a recorded workspace); LRU evicts hidden AUX only — session windows are NEVER closed by the stage (userW's killed-session lesson); desktop autosave is SUPPRESSED while staged (layout.js gate) — stage state never leaks into desktop records; userW's four defenses ported: serialized latest-wins switches, keyed replay dedup 15s, programmatic-switch guard, separate _hiddenByStage flag. RESTORATION MATRIX (2.112.1-2, design §4b, all 17 openSpec actions): durable=file/info-probed then replayed; derived=archive-entry temps carry via:{archive,entry} recipe and RE-EXTRACT on replay (the zip-PDF case); volatile=blob/data pages+recipe-less temps+DIRTY EDITORS (winInfo._editorDirty(), CodeEditor.modified exposed 2.112.2) are NEVER LRU-evicted and skip+one-toast when dead at replay; deleted task groups + 404 workflows pre-checked. INIT-ORDER GOTCHA (2.112.3, real 'placeholder never moves' report): stage.init() MUST run after initStateSync — StateSync.set() SILENTLY DROPS writes for unregistered stores, so the slot never persisted while dragging worked fine; _sync() lazily registers the store on any access as the guard. Any new SyncStore consumer needs the same care. **2.112.4 field fixes (4 user reports, CDP-smoke-verified — do not regress):** ① leave()/_deactivateHero HAND THE HERO BACK to the desktop system — restore _stageHomeBounds→gridBounds, clear _isStageHero/_onStage (off-stage moves edit HOME bounds, not the slot), then dm._hideWin/target-show like a normal desktop window (bare _hideStage left it invisible on its home desktop AND at slot size — the reported bug); enter() re-borrows via the still-set _heroWinId with a FRESH home snapshot; STAGE_ID-created heroes have no home and stay stage-hidden; _materializeInner clears stale _hiddenByDesktop. ② stage grid persists via stage-store key 'grid' — the interception lives at the TOP of scheduleAutoSave (NOT _doAutoSave: the _restoring gates dropped the call before the stage gate ran, smoke-caught); enter() applies it. ③ stage↔desktop window drags blocked BOTH directions (user directive superseding the design-§4 unbind+move): stage.dragToDesktopBlocked(win) gates window.js preview hover + dm.moveWindowToDesktop (covers the right-click menu too); placeholder additionally healStray()-recaptured + excluded from captureState (real report: escaped placeholder stuck on a desktop). ④ desktop.dynamicEnabled applies LIVE (init() settings listener → render kick / leave-if-disabled); same class fixed in sidebar for sessionCard.* card-build-time settings — a liveApply:true schema flag is a CLAIM, every render-time-read setting needs a change listener that re-renders its surface. **2.112.5 MULTI-CLIENT (real cross-client window-loss report — do not regress):** materializing a session with NO local window used to mint a stage-owned hero under a FRESH winId; leaving to the desktop holding that session per OTHER clients' records captured a state missing the recorded winId → the broadcast CLOSED the window on every client. Fix = IDENTITY ADOPTION: `_adoptDesktopIdentity` scans dm._savedStates for the session and converges (wm.rekeyWindow onto the recorded winId — chain-less windows only — + home desktop/gridBounds/isMaximized), retried at leave/_deactivateHero for late ids (resume fills openSpec async). Structural gotchas: createWindow's TAIL focus runs materialization BEFORE app.js's desktop-tag wrapper assigns _desktopId (adoption gate accepts !win._desktopId; the wrapper must not clobber an id set during creation); maximized heroes un-maximize onto the slot and hand-back applies home element geometry BEFORE re-maximizing (toggleMaximize must record HOME px as prevBounds). Live sync: init() subscribes 'stage'/'*' (StateSync fires for REMOTE ops only) — slot → re-apply to occupant, grid → setGrid, ws:<activeHeroKey> → debounced _reconcileWorkspace (restore + close-absent aux, dirty editors exempt); _scheduleRecord(500ms) on aux create/move keeps records live. **Hero is SHARED across clients (2.112.6, user directive — 挂机/walk-over): 'hero' store key {key, openSpec}; _publishHero at materialize (skip while _applyingRemoteHero), _followRemoteHero on remote ops (pointer-down deferred) + at enter (shared hero WINS over the tab's stale hero); hero close → clear → placeholder everywhere; only staged-or-not stays per-tab. closeWindow auto-focus-next (and applyLayout/overlap filters) must skip _hiddenByStage — a stage-hidden previous hero got auto-focused on hero close and re-materialized fleet-wide (harness-caught).** **2.151.0 preview fixes (2nd ghost report — do not regress):** ① the 2.134.0 home-bounds substitution in dm._renderSwitcher read `_stageHomeBounds?.gridBounds` but _stageHomeBounds IS the flat bounds object → always undefined → hero ghost-painted at the SLOT position on its home preview for 17 releases (shape-mismatch no-op; now reads the flat shape); ② the preview render DIGEST must cover every field the render filters on — _hiddenByDesktop/_hiddenByStage/_onStage were missing, so leave()'s intermediate setGrid-triggered render (target windows still hidden) cached a BLANK preview and the post-show render early-returned (preview stayed white until visiting another desktop); leave() also mirrors switchTo's +400/+1300ms refreshSwitcher retries. COMMITTED smoke: scripts/test-stage-preview.mjs (throwaway git-worktree server + headless-chrome raw CDP, 9 assertions through the REAL materialize/leave paths — replaces the ephemeral harnesses). Verified: 2-CDP-client harness 10/10 + single-client 11/11 — harness needs --disable-background-timer-throttling + real per-client input for _userDirty. **2.209.0 pile-at-slot rootfix (userW's 超级重叠 — Stage↔desktop round trip stacked every stage-born session at the slot, accumulating per trip; repro'd 16/16 then fixed, scripts/test-stage-overlap.mjs): ① enter() re-shows ONLY the live workspace (remembered hero + aux bound to _heroKey/'__pending__') — the blanket every-_hiddenByStage re-show resurrected every slot-PARKED ex-hero; ② _borrowHero ALWAYS creates a home snapshot BEFORE applying the slot (a window born while staged has no gridBounds at the synchronous creation-tail borrow → pixel _captureGridBounds first — safe, stage flags not yet set — else _cascadeHome(); slot-near bounds never become home) and _handBackHero has a cascade belt for pre-fix degenerated field state — without this the slot was the window's ONLY geometry forever; ③ dm.switchTo routes through stage.leave()/enter() while staged and treats '__stage__' as never-a-target — a raw switchTo (add-desktop +, command-mode d/D, moveSessionWindow, _onRemoteDesktopUpdated) captured the stage's window set under desktops['__stage__'] (lazy-replayed later as REAL windows at slot bounds) and left stage._active desynced; client boot + server layout-sync both refuse-and-scrub the poisoned record; ④ _onRemoteDesktopUpdated exempts STAGE_ID windows from deleted-desktop reassignment (any remote desktop meta change used to retag the whole stage onto a normal desktop) and never force-switches a staged client; ⑤ _materializeInner aborts when !_active (queued materialize firing post-leave); ⑥ leave() validates the target desktop still exists (remote-deleted _prevDesktopId). KNOWN MINOR (parked): stale shared-hero follow — _publishHero early-returns on null _heroKey with no retry at id adoption, so multi-client walk-over can follow an outdated hero record.**

### session-palette.js

    session-palette.js — Ctrl+K session palette (fuzzy session switcher; installSessionPalette, desktop only). **SEARCH IS TOKENISED (2.249.1, userW inc-msjro90z-n6y3 — never regress to whole-query matching):** the query splits on whitespace with AND semantics; NAME hits (1000-pos) outrank path/host/id hits (300) outrank a fuzzy subsequence (100) CONFINED TO THE NAME. Matching the query as ONE literal string meant a space could never substring-hit a CamelCase/hyphenated name ("best ever" vs "BestEver-ToB-signing"), so EVERY multi-word query fell through to a subsequence over the whole haystack (name + cwd + 3 UUIDs) — a shared cwd prefix supplies almost any letter sequence, so unrelated sessions all tied at the same low score. Compounding it, the live bonus was `+1000` on that tie, sorting every live session above every stopped one, and `slice(0,12)` cut the list before the wanted STOPPED session could appear. Ranking is now `sc*1000 + (live?500:0)` — match quality dominates, live only breaks ties among equally-good matches. Fuzzy-matching against a UUID is pure noise; never re-add it. Test: scripts/test-session-palette-search.mjs (11 asserts on the real reported data).


### collab-row.js (PURE, B-7473)

  src/collab-row.js — the label/HTML builder for codex multi-agent collab rows. PURE tier, **zero requires** (CJS pulled into the bundle like ssh-key-format.js): the escaper, the translator and the icon set are INJECTED. That is not ceremony — it makes the XSS rule *verifiable*: scripts/test-codex-subagents ① passes a MARKER escaper and asserts that nothing outside the markers is model-controlled, then repeats with the real `escHtml` semantics. `collabRowParts` splits a row into `{pre, name, post}` so the plain-text label (which lands in the message's `tool_result` output → search previews, fold summaries, the minimap) and the HTML row can never drift; `collabRowsHtml` renders one row or the coalesced summary + one clickable chip per agent; `collabRowTitle` is the hover envelope and says *payload encrypted upstream* when the body was withheld. A row NEVER carries an encrypted blob. The default translator substitutes `{n}` so server-side text is complete without a client `t()`. **LIVE PROGRESS READOUT (2026-09-07, owner: “这种互聊如果连续发生是不是应该界面里展示下连续数量，这样我好知道对话没卡住”):** dozens of ENCRYPTED one-line rows over minutes with no assistant text between them are indistinguishable from a wedged turn, so the module gained the composers every progress surface reads — `collabTrafficStats` (count / distinct agents / first+last row `ts` / `messagesOnly`, i.e. every row is mail vs a mixed set that must say “sub-agent events”), `collabAgeText` (1s granularity under a minute, whole FLOORED minutes above — “is it moving”, not a stopwatch), `collabSpanText` (“4 min 12 s”), `collabCountParts` (REAL singular keys — never “1 messages”), `collabHeadText`, `collabRunPart` (the run-label segment) and `subAgentStreamLabel` (the spinner line). NOTHING is stored and nothing is counted into a field: every number is derived from the rows the normalizer already stamped, so a second client renders identical text off the same message state. LIVE and FROZEN differ ONLY in the last segment — a ticking `last {age} ago` while the turn runs, the absolute `over {span}` once it stops — and a row with no usable `ts` DROPS the segment rather than inventing a clock (never “over 0 s”). `collabRowsHtml` puts the head in its OWN `.chat-collab-head` element so the view's single ticker rewrites just that text (re-rendering the card would drop the fold state the user opened), and a LONE row still renders its full `agent · TYPE` label — summarising one event only loses information, and the owner's ask is about CONTINUOUS chatter, which is the coalesced shape by construction. `collabSummaryText` (the stored `tool_result` output that search previews and the minimap read) is the SAME composer in its frozen form, so the words on screen and the words in the index cannot drift. `collabRowTitle` now leads with `Time: <clock> (record timestamp|arrival time)`: the normalizer's `_stampRowTime` takes the RECORD's own timestamp when it has one (`recordTs`, which returns null instead of `toTs`'s silent `Date.now()`) and the arrival time otherwise — saying WHICH is the point, or a burst replayed from a rebuild would read as “just now”. **THREE SURFACES = THREE SCOPES (2026-09-07 r2, and they are MEANT to differ):** `collabHeadText` counts ONE coalesced card, `collabRunPart` the folded RUN, `subAgentStreamLabel` the whole TURN — a card that stopped growing at 4 events sits inside a run of 5 while the spinner counts the turn's 5, so all three are honest and only the one that floats FREE of the thing it counts has to say which scope it means (“Sub-agents working — 5 sub-agent events **this turn**, last 4s ago”); the other two are read inside their own container. Do not "fix" the divergence by making the card head turn-scoped — two cards would then both claim the turn's total. Gate: scripts/test-collab-live-counter.mjs ①②.

### chat-view.js
  chat-view.js — ChatView controller (virtual scroll, op dispatch, lifecycle; gap-seek mixin in chat-view-seek.js). FOLD KINDS (2.369.33; chat.collapseKinds, semantic, one set for every backend): thinking/bash/read/write/memory/mcp/skill/agent/search/image — memberKind() maps claude tool names (Bash→bash, Read→read, Write/Edit/Patch→write, Grep/Glob/LS→read, WebSearch/WebFetch→search, Read of an image file→image (2.369.34), ToolSearch→mcp, Skill→skill, Agent/Task→agent, mcp__*→mcp) while codex/ACP cards arrive with `collapseKind` stamped by their normalizer (codex web_search/web_fetch→search, view_image→image; ACP kind search→search). The run header's byKind counter must list EVERY kind the classifier returns — an unlisted kind counts NaN and vanishes from the summary (2.369.34). INVARIANT: a tool card whose kind resolves to null is not merely unfolded — it BREAKS the run around it (the 42-WebSearch session had nothing folded); every new tool name needs a kind. Users who customised the kinds list must tick a new kind themselves (sparse settings keep their explicit list). **MSG-META POPUP (2026-09-06, codex per-message meta):** `_showMsgMeta` is backend-neutral — additive rows only when the meta carries them: 'Reasoning tokens' (`usage.reasoning_output_tokens`), 'Effort' (`meta.effort`), the cache-write row falls back to codex's `usage.cache_write_input_tokens` when there is no claude `cache_creation` TTL split (claude usage never has that field ⇒ unchanged), and the id labels follow the normalizer's kind markers — `requestIdKind:'ledger'` ⇒ 'Ledger request key' (the cx synthetic key is never dressed as a vendor request id), `msgIdKind:'response'` ⇒ 'Response ID'; claude meta has no kinds ⇒ old labels. The billing row's `sessionBilling()` fallback used to read `auth.accountName`/`auth.kind` — fields the server's sessionAuth shape ({source,name,poolTarget,tail,detail,hostName}) never had, so it answered null for EVERY backend; it now reads `source` like session-props/session-card (pooled → "name → target", codex-subscription → name, codex-cli → 'ChatGPT login', subscription → name|'CLI login', api-console/api-key/api-other) and the local 'not in the ledger yet' state is prefixed with that identity; a found global-bucket event names the login of ITS harness (`r.be === 'codex'` → 'ChatGPT login', the route returns `be`/`model`/`effort` additively). The run header's per-kind summary ("N web searches") is the fold's ONLY search-specific text — the query itself lives on the card title (chat-renderers, below), not here. FOLD KINDS (2.369.33; chat.collapseKinds, semantic, one set for every backend): thinking/bash/read/write/memory/mcp/skill/agent/search/image — memberKind() maps claude tool names (Bash→bash, Read→read, Write/Edit/Patch→write, Grep/Glob/LS→read, WebSearch/WebFetch→search, Read of an image file→image (2.369.34), ToolSearch→**lookup** (2.369.37 — NOT mcp: the owner caught "1 次 MCP" over a run whose only non-Bash card was a ToolSearch; lookups fold under the MCP toggle via `foldToggleFor` but are labelled "N tool lookups", and the "(server)" suffix comes only from real mcp__ calls), Skill→skill, Agent/Task→agent, mcp__*→mcp) while codex/ACP cards arrive with `collapseKind` stamped by their normalizer (codex web_search/web_fetch→search, view_image→image; ACP kind search→search). **Since 2.369.37 the classifier + label live in the PURE module `src/lib/chat-run-summary.js`** (`messageKind`, `foldToggleFor`, `countKinds`, `runSummaryParts`, `runSummaryLabel`, `mcpParts` — chat-renderers re-exports that one mcpParts): `countKinds` zero-fills `RUN_KINDS` and test-fold-ux asserts `SUMMARY_ORDER` covers every kind, so the 2.369.34 class (an unlisted kind counted NaN and vanished) is structural. INVARIANT: a tool card whose kind resolves to null is not merely unfolded — it BREAKS the run around it (the 42-WebSearch session had nothing folded); every new tool name needs a kind. Users who customised the kinds list must tick a new kind themselves (sparse settings keep their explicit list). **RUN CHROME (2.369.37, expanded-run legibility — see kb-features Chat Mode for the DOM shape):** `_updateRuns` rebuilds `this._runs` = `[{header, members, footer, label, open}]` every pass; `_setRunOpen(run, open)` is the ONE writer of `.chat-run-collapsed` / `.chat-run-member` / `-first` / `-last` and of the `.chat-run-footer` (inserted after the last member only while open); header AND footer are non-`.chat-msg` list children that every pass removes (`:scope > .chat-run-header, :scope > .chat-run-footer`) — so `_withViewportAnchor` and the seek gap anchor SKIP them as anchor candidates (a run-chrome anchor is dead after any pass inside fn → estimate-skewed delta fallback; members are only class-toggled and stay stable). The floating `.chat-run-bar` (lazily created on the `.chat-view` container, never in the list) is updated by `_updateRunBar(scrollTop)` from the scroll handler's rAF — placed BEFORE the `_programmaticScroll` early return, with the frame's already-read scrollTop, header/footer offsets read in the same layout pass; `_scheduleRunBar` (rAF-coalesced) re-runs it after each pass / setRunOpen / resume; `setSuspended(true)` hides it; dispose cancels the rAF. Bar/footer collapse = `_collapseRunTo` → `_setRunOpen(false)` + `_landOnHeader` (absolute `scrollTop = header.offsetTop` under a 400ms `_programmaticScroll` mute via `_jumpGuardTimer`, `_lastStructuralAt` stamped with dir null, pin re-derived from the landing). Toggling a run outside a pass mutates childList (footer) → the observer schedules a pass, whose anchor restore lands on the same position (it anchors on the first non-chrome visible element). Rules that must hold: the bar never calls `_extendTop/_extendBottom/_trim*` or writes `_pinned` (test-fold-ux greps its body); no Esc binding (data-popover protocol); the footer never counts toward the rendered window (trims select `.chat-msg:not(.chat-gap-msg)`). FOLD KINDS (2.369.33; chat.collapseKinds, semantic, one set for every backend): thinking/bash/read/write/memory/mcp/skill/agent/search/image — memberKind() maps claude tool names (Bash→bash, Read→read, Write/Edit/Patch→write, Grep/Glob/LS→read, WebSearch/WebFetch→search, Read of an image file→image (2.369.34), ToolSearch→**lookup** (2.369.37 — NOT mcp: the owner caught "1 次 MCP" over a run whose only non-Bash card was a ToolSearch; lookups fold under the MCP toggle via `foldToggleFor` but are labelled "N tool lookups", and the "(server)" suffix comes only from real mcp__ calls), Skill→skill, Agent/Task→agent, mcp__*→mcp) while codex/ACP cards arrive with `collapseKind` stamped by their normalizer (codex web_search/web_fetch→search, view_image→image; ACP kind search→search). **Since 2.369.37 the classifier + label live in the PURE module `src/lib/chat-run-summary.js`** (`messageKind`, `foldToggleFor`, `countKinds`, `runSummaryParts`, `runSummaryLabel`, `mcpParts` — chat-renderers re-exports that one mcpParts): `countKinds` zero-fills `RUN_KINDS` and test-fold-ux asserts `SUMMARY_ORDER` covers every kind, so the 2.369.34 class (an unlisted kind counted NaN and vanished) is structural. INVARIANT: a tool card whose kind resolves to null is not merely unfolded — it BREAKS the run around it (the 42-WebSearch session had nothing folded); every new tool name needs a kind. Users who customised the kinds list must tick a new kind themselves (sparse settings keep their explicit list). **RUN CHROME (2.369.37, expanded-run legibility — see kb-features Chat Mode for the DOM shape):** `_updateRuns` rebuilds `this._runs` = `[{header, members, footer, label, open}]` every pass; `_setRunOpen(run, open)` is the ONE writer of `.chat-run-collapsed` / `.chat-run-member` / `-first` / `-last` and of the `.chat-run-footer` (inserted after the last member only while open); header AND footer are non-`.chat-msg` list children that every pass removes (`:scope > .chat-run-header, :scope > .chat-run-footer`) — so `_withViewportAnchor` and the seek gap anchor SKIP them as anchor candidates (a run-chrome anchor is dead after any pass inside fn → estimate-skewed delta fallback; members are only class-toggled and stay stable). The floating `.chat-run-bar` (lazily created on the `.chat-view` container, never in the list) is updated by `_updateRunBar(scrollTop)` from the scroll handler's rAF — placed BEFORE the `_programmaticScroll` early return, with the frame's already-read scrollTop, header/footer offsets read in the same layout pass; `_scheduleRunBar` (rAF-coalesced) re-runs it after each pass / setRunOpen / resume; `setSuspended(true)` hides it; dispose cancels the rAF. Bar/footer collapse = `_collapseRunTo` → `_setRunOpen(false)` + `_landOnHeader` (absolute `scrollTop = header.offsetTop` under a 400ms `_programmaticScroll` mute via `_jumpGuardTimer`, `_lastStructuralAt` stamped with dir null, pin re-derived from the landing). **Toggling a run OUTSIDE a pass is itself a childList mutation (the footer) and must be hidden from the observer exactly the way a pass drains its own records** (2026-09-06 adversarial review, measured in headless chrome: on a PINNED view with 2+ runs, clicking any header except the last opened the run and re-collapsed it ~180ms later). A user click is not a pass, so the footer record scheduled one, and that pass's PINNED AUTO-REFOLD (`built.slice(0, -1)` — while `_pinned`, only the LAST run keeps an open flag, so a run expanded to watch the live tail re-folds once the tail moves past it, 2.227.x “一部分没折叠”) closed the run the click had just opened: every non-last header was a no-op while pinned. `_setRunOpen` now sets `_runsMutating` around the footer insert/remove when called outside a pass and calls `takeRecords()` only if it REALLY mutated (never swallow an unrelated pending record), and a deliberate toggle on a NON-LAST run marks its members in `_runStickyOpen` — a WeakSet keyed by member element like `_runExpanded`, because run records are rebuilt every pass; the auto-refold skips marked runs and nothing else, collapsing (header/footer/bar) clears the mark, and an INHERITED open flag still re-folds. The anchor restore is unaffected (it anchors on the first non-chrome visible element). Overlay hygiene: the floating bar shares the list's top edge with `.chat-pos-indicator` AND the history-load pill `.chat-history-status` — both are positioned by chat.css (the load pill lost its inline `top`, which no selector could have overridden) and both drop to `top:34px` (42px ≤768px) under `.chat-view.chat-run-bar-on`, the transient overlap being paging up INSIDE an open run. **Fixture law learned here: a browser fixture with ONE run cannot reach the multi-run branches** — 51 asserts were green over a broken interaction; test-fold-ux now builds two runs (the long tool run + a short mcp run after a text turn) and pins first-run-stays-open at t+600ms and across an explicit pass, the last run's unchanged behaviour, and the inherited-flag negative control. Rules that must hold: the bar never calls `_extendTop/_extendBottom/_trim*` or writes `_pinned` (test-fold-ux greps its body); no Esc binding (data-popover protocol); the footer never counts toward the rendered window (trims select `.chat-msg:not(.chat-gap-msg)`). **SHORT-VIEW RESCUE after attach (2.369.43)**: content that doesn't fill the viewport has no scrollable range, so the scroll handler never runs and touch has no wheel-up path — a fold-dominated slab (50 cards collapsed into two run headers) is a dead end with thousands of unreachable messages above. `_shortViewNeedsFill(list)` is the decision (more history above, no scrollable range, not suspended/disposed/teleported, and `rendered + 50 <= 150` so the extra page can never trip a trim onto the live tail) and `_scheduleAttachFill()` takes TWO readings 700ms/1600ms after loadHistory, extending only if both agree and `_lastStructuralAt` didn't move between them — the collapsed-geometry artifact that motivated the 2.369.36 hardening is transient and cannot survive the second reading. The 2.369.36 form (`rendered < 30`) was UNSATISFIABLE — every attach ships tail(50) and folding hides members without removing them, so the rescue was dead code; both timers clear in dispose(). Because chat-view.js is DOM-free at import, the predicate + schedule are FUNCTIONALLY tested against fake geometry in scripts/test-chat-trim-guard.mjs. A Read is 'image' only when its RESULT carried image blocks (`content[0].images`) — EVIDENCE, never the extension (image-card review round 2: 12/12 real `Read *.svg` results in the fleet are numbered TEXT, and an extension rule both mis-counted them and let them escape the fold); codex/ACP arrive with `collapseKind` stamped. **THE IMAGE MEMBER IS EXEMPT FROM THE FOLD, NOT THE RUN (2.369.43 → image-card review round 2):** 'image' ships ON in the default kinds and the "any tool card folds, even a lone one" rule applied to it too, so every media card landed inside "1 image read" — `display:none`, invisible until clicked, and its `loading="lazy"` thumbnail never even fetched (the owner asked to SEE the image). The first cut exempted whole runs made ONLY of image views, which is the RARE shape — in real sessions the card sits BETWEEN foldable cards (Bash → Read(png) → Bash) and stayed hidden. So `flush()` computes `run.inline` = the image-kind members: they stay members of the run for every other purpose (rail, first/last, sticky mark, the floating bar's span, the summary's "N image reads") but `_setRunOpen` never gives them `.chat-run-collapsed`, so the fold closes AROUND them. A run with nothing left to fold (every member an image) gets no header at all — a fold control that hides nothing is a dead control. Pinned by test-image-cards ⑦ + the test-fold-ux browser fixture (bash|image|bash: the media card's computed display is not 'none' and its `<img>` really loads, while the Bash cards fold under the header). MEDIA THUMBNAILS: the list carries a CAPTURE-phase `error` delegate (img error does not bubble) that flags a broken `.chat-tool-img`'s `.chat-media` as `.chat-media-broken` (CSS swaps the img for the honest line) — rendered HTML never carries inline handlers; the fold's `display:none` is what keeps `loading="lazy"` thumbnails of a collapsed run from fetching. **COLLAB CLICK-THROUGH (B-7473):** a message carrying `collab` renders through chat-renderers `_renderCollabMsg` (report card / compact rows, src/collab-row.js does the escaping); the agent NAME (`.chat-collab-name`, data-agent-path + data-thread-id) is delegated in the messageList click handler to `_openCollabAgent`, which opens the child's rollout in the EXISTING sub-agent viewer when the thread id is known (0.153.4 always knows it — SubAgentActivity.agent_thread_id) and otherwise asks `GET /api/subagents?backend=codex&threadId=<root>` and TOASTS when the child is not on this machine (never a silent no-op). The run header gains `{n} sub-agent messages` (counting `collab.rows[].dir === 'in'`) and a `{n} sub-agents: …` chip list built from the run's rows — those chips get their OWN onclick with `stopPropagation()`, because the header's click toggles the fold, and they de-duplicate on `row.agentPath`, which the normalizer normalises to an ABSOLUTE path for every direction (round-5 fix: outbound rows keyed the bare `task_name` and one agent drew two chips). **The two B-7473 summary lines live in the PURE module like every other line (integration 2026-09-06 + round-5 rebase):** `'report'` — a sub-agent's written answer — is a REAL fold kind in `RUN_KINDS`/`SUMMARY_ORDER` (offered in `chat.collapseKinds` but shipping UNCHECKED, because a report is the content the window was opened to read; the one-line rows stay `'agent'`, which IS default-on), and the inbound-message count is `SUMMARY_EXTRAS = ['subAgentIn']` — a count the CALLER assigns into `byKind` (never produced by `messageKind`, never zero-filled, so an unset one renders nothing and can never go NaN) whose position in the label is decided by the same single `SUMMARY_ORDER`. chat-view NEVER keeps a second kind table. **THE QUEUE CHIP'S CAPABILITY ARRIVES AFTER THE PIXELS (2026-09-06, round-3 review):** the chip on a user bubble is built by `renderUserMsg`, which asks the view (`getQueueCaps` → `_queueCaps()` = harness `inputModes` ∧ the RUNNING wrapper's `_queueSupported`) and hands `applyQueueChip` a null handler when the answer is no — which renders it `disabled`. `_queueSupported` starts FALSE and BOTH of its sources land AFTER the bubbles: `loadHistory` renders every message and calls `_applyLiveMeta` (the attach payload's `queueSupported`) at the END, and a live wrapper's baseline `queue_changed` (`_onMeta` subtype 'queue', `supported:true`) arrives frames after the first messages. So after ANY history load the chip was permanently dead. `_setQueueSupported` is therefore the ONE writer of the flag and a change in EITHER direction schedules `_refreshQueueChips()` — rAF-coalesced, ONE pass over `_elements`, re-running `ChatRenderers.applyQueueChip` for every rendered message that carries a `queueState` (false→true makes them live, true→false makes them inert; the pending frame is cancelled in dispose()). The queue STRIP never had this bug because `_setQueue` re-renders it — the chips live inside bubbles nobody rebuilds. Gate: test-queue-steer ⑦ (render-then-deliver for both messengers + the reverse flip + a one-writer pin). **AUTO-SCROLL IS AUTOMATIC REPOSITIONING, AND IT MUST LOSE TO THE READER (B-9702, round 5 of the desktop-resume campaign):** `_forceScrollToBottom` writes `scrollTop = scrollHeight` for up to 10 frames (~166 ms) under the `_programmaticScroll` mute — the pinned view's auto-follow AND what every resume re-tail rung calls. It now carries an EPOCH: `_cancelForcedScroll(via)` bumps it and is called from `_notePositioning` and `_noteUserNav` (never from `_noteUserInput` — a click moves nothing), so every frame already queued is orphaned instead of writing scrollTop once more, while a later `_forceScrollToBottom` starts a fresh epoch so a cancel can never kill auto-follow. The mute is released ONE FRAME after the final write, guarded on `_fsbActive`: a scroll event is delivered after the callback that wrote it, so clearing the flag in the write's own frame handed our displacement to the boundary decision as if a reader had produced it. Without both, a re-tail rung armed at resume+1240 ms was still writing when the reader wheeled up at +1400 ms, its last frame landed 3 ms after their scroll, the pin re-engaged off our own write (`repin st:1760 … fsb:-1 posAgo:3` — ordinary geometry, `collapsedGeomSkip` never fired) and `_extendTop`'s pinned-tail invariant re-asserted the bottom: 5 of 20 real wheel-ups undone, 0 of 20 after. The `repin` trace (the twin of `unpin`) is what named it and stays. Gates: scripts/test-chat-trim-guard.mjs (DOM-free chain semantics against a fake rAF queue, including the "auto-follow survives a cancel" and "the mute is never permanent" controls) + the two +1400 ms wheel legs in scripts/test-desktop-resume-paging.mjs (the second arms a chain by hand for determinism, with `_cancelForcedScroll` neutered on the instance as its control). **LIVE SUB-AGENT TRAFFIC (2026-09-07, the same owner ask):** three surfaces read the ONE composer over three SCOPES — card / run / turn (they can legitimately show different numbers at the same instant; see collab-row.js, where the free-floating one names its scope). (i) the coalesced card's head — the renderer asks the view `isCollabLive(msg)` → `_liveCollabId()`, which is the LAST message and only while `_typingSince` is set (a view-only window has no live turn, so a stopped transcript is always frozen; the moment anything else arrives the burst is over and the card freezes). (ii) the run header / footer / floating bar — `flush()` computes `collabStats` over the run's `collab.rows` and captures `mkLabel({now, live})`, a CLOSURE over that run's own counts, so the per-second repaint re-asks the ONE composer (`runSummaryLabel` gained a PRE-COMPOSED `collabPart` string — words from src/collab-row.js, POSITION from chat-run-summary.js, which still imports nothing) instead of rebuilding a second kind table; the “{n} sub-agents” text moved OUT of `chat-run-agents` into the label so the floating bar and the footer carry it too and the header never says it twice, leaving only the clickable chips there — with an explicit ` · ` in front of them (r2: dropping the old “{n} sub-agents:” colon glued the count to the names, “2 sub-agent events water_research” read as one phrase). (iii) the spinner line — `_noteRecordKind` (called from `_onOp` AFTER the message is applied, so a coalescing edit carries the grown rows) records whether the newest typed record was collab traffic, and `_applyStreamLabel` shows `subAgentStreamLabel(_liveCollabStats())` — TURN-scoped, because a report card between two coalesced cards would restart a per-card count mid-burst. THE SERVER STAYS THE LABEL AUTHORITY: `_onServerStreamLabel` is the one place that remembers the broadcast label/kind (all three call sites — the `streaming-label` frame, the attach meta, the reattach reply — go through it), and the override yields back ONLY what it took (`_collabLabelShown`); re-asserting the remembered label on every op would fight the label ChatInput sets locally on send and a stale “running Bash” from the previous turn would win. THE TICKER: ONE `setInterval(1000)` per ChatView, created lazily on the first collab record, bound to `winInfo._listenerCtl.signal` ('abort' → `_stopCollabTick`) and cleared in `dispose()` — a per-message interval over a fifty-row burst is fifty timers nobody cancels. It is a NO-OP while `_suspended` (a hidden window's ages are unread; `setSuspended(false)` runs one tick so the first frame back is right), it writes ONLY `textContent` into the head / header / footer / bar (never `_extendTop`/`_trim*`/`_pinned`/`scrollTop` — the suite greps its body), and it FREEZES every card the traffic has moved past exactly once (`_freezeStaleHeads(liveId, now)`). `_hideTyping` → `_freezeCollab()`: turn end stops the ticker and repaints every surface in its absolute-span form, because a ticking age on a finished turn reads as progress. **THE FREEZE MUST NOT DEPEND ON THE TICKER HAVING PAINTED (r2 MAJOR, reproduced 2/2):** the first cut repainted only the card the TICKER had last touched, and a burst that starts AND ends while the window is hidden (desktop switch — start an orchestration, switch desktop, come back) never ticks at all, while the RENDERER keeps painting each coalescing edit with a live age; the card then read “last 0s ago” on a turn that had ended minutes ago, contradicting the run header beside it (whose liveness IS captured in the fold pass, which runs while hidden). So the authority is a SET: `_noteCollabHeadPainted` (the renderer's `isCollabLive` hook returns its answer through it, and every `_paintCollabHead` keeps it honest — a card trimmed out of the window just leaves) and `_freezeStaleHeads(liveId|null, now)` walks it, from the tick AND from the freeze. ARMING: `_startCollabTick` is gated on `_liveCollabId()` (a claude session must not carry an interval at all) and paints ONCE immediately — `loadHistory` renders every card BEFORE `_showTyping` runs, so a window attached mid-burst would otherwise sit on the frozen span; `_onServerStreamLabel` is the event that finally makes liveness answerable, so it arms the ticker too (attaching to a STALLED turn is precisely the case the readout exists for).
